<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">User Notice</h1><a id="user-content-user-notice" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: User Notice" href="#user-notice"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The default page of the repo is on <code>dev</code> branch. To install the dev version, please check the section <code>Installing from Dev Branch</code>. If you are looking for a <strong>stable version</strong>, please refer to the <code>master</code> branch <a href="https://github.com/uber/orbit/tree/master">here</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Disclaimer</h1><a id="user-content-disclaimer" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Disclaimer" href="#disclaimer"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This project</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>is stable and being incubated for long-term support. It may contain new experimental code, for which APIs are subject to change.</li>
<li>requires <a href="https://mc-stan.org/cmdstanpy/" rel="nofollow">cmdstanpy</a> as one of the core dependencies for Bayesian sampling.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Orbit: A Python Package for Bayesian Forecasting</h1><a id="user-content-orbit-a-python-package-for-bayesian-forecasting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Orbit: A Python Package for Bayesian Forecasting" href="#orbit-a-python-package-for-bayesian-forecasting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Orbit is a Python package for Bayesian time series forecasting and inference. It provides a
familiar and intuitive initialize-fit-predict interface for time series tasks, while utilizing probabilistic programming languages under the hood.</p>
<p dir="auto">For details, check out our documentation and tutorials:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>HTML (stable): <a href="https://orbit-ml.readthedocs.io/en/stable/" rel="nofollow">https://orbit-ml.readthedocs.io/en/stable/</a></li>
<li>HTML (latest): <a href="https://orbit-ml.readthedocs.io/en/latest/" rel="nofollow">https://orbit-ml.readthedocs.io/en/latest/</a></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Currently, it supports concrete implementations for the following models:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Exponential Smoothing (ETS)</li>
<li>Local Global Trend (LGT)</li>
<li>Damped Local Trend (DLT)</li>
<li>Kernel Time-based Regression (KTR)</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">It also supports the following sampling/optimization methods for model estimation/inferences:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Markov-Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) as a full sampling method</li>
<li>Maximum a Posteriori (MAP) as a point estimate method</li>
<li>Variational Inference (VI) as a hybrid-sampling method on approximate
distribution</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installing Stable Release</h3><a id="user-content-installing-stable-release" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installing Stable Release" href="#installing-stable-release"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Install the library either from PyPi or from the source with <code>pip</code>.
Alternatively, you can also install it from Anaconda with <code>conda</code>:</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>With pip</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Installing from PyPI</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ pip install orbit-ml"><pre>$ pip install orbit-ml</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Install from source</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ git clone https://github.com/uber/orbit.git
$ cd orbit
$ pip install -r requirements.txt
$ pip install ."><pre>$ git clone https://github.com/uber/orbit.git
$ <span class="pl-c1">cd</span> orbit
$ pip install -r requirements.txt
$ pip install <span class="pl-c1">.</span></pre></div>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>With conda</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">The library can be installed from the conda-forge channel using conda.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ conda install -c conda-forge orbit-ml"><pre>$ conda install -c conda-forge orbit-ml</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installing from Dev Branch</h3><a id="user-content-installing-from-dev-branch" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installing from Dev Branch" href="#installing-from-dev-branch"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ pip install git+https://github.com/uber/orbit.git@dev"><pre>$ pip install git+https://github.com/uber/orbit.git@dev</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start with Damped-Local-Trend (DLT) Model</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start-with-damped-local-trend-dlt-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start with Damped-Local-Trend (DLT) Model" href="#quick-start-with-damped-local-trend-dlt-model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">FULL Bayesian Prediction</h3><a id="user-content-full-bayesian-prediction" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: FULL Bayesian Prediction" href="#full-bayesian-prediction"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from orbit.utils.dataset import load_iclaims
from orbit.models import DLT
from orbit.diagnostics.plot import plot_predicted_data

# log-transformed data
df = load_iclaims()
# train-test split
test_size = 52
train_df = df[:-test_size]
test_df = df[-test_size:]

dlt = DLT(
  response_col='claims', date_col='week',
  regressor_col=['trend.unemploy', 'trend.filling', 'trend.job'],
  seasonality=52,
)
dlt.fit(df=train_df)

# outcomes data frame
predicted_df = dlt.predict(df=test_df)

plot_predicted_data(
  training_actual_df=train_df, predicted_df=predicted_df,
  date_col=dlt.date_col, actual_col=dlt.response_col,
  test_actual_df=test_df
)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">orbit</span>.<span class="pl-s1">utils</span>.<span class="pl-s1">dataset</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">load_iclaims</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">orbit</span>.<span class="pl-s1">models</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-c1">DLT</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">orbit</span>.<span class="pl-s1">diagnostics</span>.<span class="pl-s1">plot</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">plot_predicted_data</span>

<span class="pl-c"># log-transformed data</span>
<span class="pl-s1">df</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">load_iclaims</span>()
<span class="pl-c"># train-test split</span>
<span class="pl-s1">test_size</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">52</span>
<span class="pl-s1">train_df</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">df</span>[:<span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-s1">test_size</span>]
<span class="pl-s1">test_df</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">df</span>[<span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-s1">test_size</span>:]

<span class="pl-s1">dlt</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">DLT</span>(
  <span class="pl-s1">response_col</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'claims'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">date_col</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'week'</span>,
  <span class="pl-s1">regressor_col</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[<span class="pl-s">'trend.unemploy'</span>, <span class="pl-s">'trend.filling'</span>, <span class="pl-s">'trend.job'</span>],
  <span class="pl-s1">seasonality</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">52</span>,
)
<span class="pl-s1">dlt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">fit</span>(<span class="pl-s1">df</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">train_df</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># outcomes data frame</span>
<span class="pl-s1">predicted_df</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">dlt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">predict</span>(<span class="pl-s1">df</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">test_df</span>)

<span class="pl-en">plot_predicted_data</span>(
  <span class="pl-s1">training_actual_df</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">train_df</span>, <span class="pl-s1">predicted_df</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">predicted_df</span>,
  <span class="pl-s1">date_col</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">dlt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">date_col</span>, <span class="pl-s1">actual_col</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">dlt</span>.<span class="pl-c1">response_col</span>,
  <span class="pl-s1">test_actual_df</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">test_df</span>
)</pre></div>
